# Transfer handoff — Swords & Sandals II Multiplayer Foundation

## 2026-09-24 Relic evaluation cadence — RCS-03C1-A selected

The owner selected `RCS-03C1-A`: every legal non-cadence-readiness episode is
evaluation-eligible at its opening cut. There is no scheduled-gated or orphaned
readiness episode in the completed design. The relationship and its first
opportunity to manifest coincide; no later authored Bell or phase is required.
This selects eligibility only, not whether the evaluator actually invokes, who
controls it, or which contingent evidence it acquires.

At the readiness-opening cut, distinct Soul, Relic, Charm, relationship facts,
and evaluation opportunity participate **directly**; their earlier formation is
**partial**; excluding a readiness queue is **neutral/protective**; universal
immediacy is **aggregate**. The cost is no authored ready-to-listen waiting
beat. The authoritative decision record remains unchanged and implementation
remains blocked.

The next-row audit found that `RCS-03C2` still bundled invocation-control source
with participant-authorization timing. It is now a non-counting parent split
into `RCS-03C2A` (system-mandatory versus participant-contingent invocation)
and conditional `RCS-03C2B` (live-at-cut versus advance-precommitment timing).
Exact actor, consent/veto, communication, and information remain in `RCS-02`;
precommitment lock/rebinding horizon remains in `RCS-13`. The register contains
thirty-four slots. C1-A reduced `Phi_SR` from 23 to 22, and replacing one open
C2 row with two returns current `Phi_SR` to 23.

The sole active card is `RCS-03C2A`. Recommend A, automatic invocation at every
eligible opportunity: deliberate source construction and the already-selected
predicate/recurrence-control choices carry player agency, while a second
invocation command risks turning the Relic into another spell button. Present
only that A/B/C choice.

## 2026-09-24 Relic evaluation lifetime — RCS-03B1-A selected

The owner selected `RCS-03B1-A`: every Soul Relic evaluation is cut-atomic.
No player-semantic evaluation begins before its decisive cut or remains pending
across a later meaningful cut before provisional completion. Earlier bounded
`H` history may remain necessary evidence, but it is not an open attempt.
Whenever later cadence and authority choices permit invocation, one coherent
Relic-side tuple and the applicable contingent evidence atomically produce a
provisional candidate or no candidate at that cut.

`RCS-03B2`–`RCS-03B7` are pruned because pending initiation, binding,
in-flight context change, cancellation, expiry, and concurrency have no object
under A. At the decisive cut the Soul/Relic/Charm/evidence relation is
**direct**; earlier temporal continuity is **partial**; excluding pending state
is **neutral/protective**; universal cut-atomicity is **aggregate**. The
authoritative decision record remains unchanged and implementation remains
blocked.

The next-row audit found that old `RCS-03C` still bundled three independent
axes that C133/C141/C155 explicitly leave open: eligible-cut cadence,
invocation authority, and contingent `H`/`S` acquisition. The owner-visible
register now contains thirty-three slots and splits that parent into
`RCS-03C1`–`RCS-03C3`. After selecting B1, pruning its six descendants,
and replacing one C row with three, current `Phi_SR = 23` (`28 - 7 + 2`). The
owner subsequently selected `RCS-03C1-A`. **Corrected continuation:** a fourth
audit split the still-bundled invocation row into `RCS-03C2A`–`RCS-03C2B`; the
sole active card is now `RCS-03C2A`, and the current thirty-four-slot register
has `Phi_SR = 23`. Present only that A/B/C invocation-control choice.

## 2026-09-24 design-first correction — the master index must not flatten the game

The first master-index draft compressed each `SC-*` gate into closure language.
That was structurally tidy and experientially wrong: it preserved what must be
proved while muting what the player is meant to experience, the actual mechanic
that creates it, the selected design philosophy, and the sharp tradeoffs. The
owner correctly rejected that loss of descriptive power.

`docs/design/endless-progression-master-closure-index.md` now uses a mandatory
two-layer form. Every one of `SC-01`–`SC-20` carries a rich design-first card
with **What we are designing**, an explicit **Souls and Simulacra / ideal fit**
classification, **Selected foundation**, **Still unresolved**, **Closure
proof**, **Source spine**, and **State**. Closure proof is a separate layer; it
never replaces the design. The source-coverage ledger maps the old system's
executive material, §§1.1–1.7, and §§2–15 to primary and secondary homes, and
the anti-flattening audit records a 20/20 structural pass across all seven
fields. The linked old prose remains part of each gate: omission from the index
cannot delete or weaken it.

The **Souls and Simulacra** identity applies now, not as a later coat of paint.
The later standalone retain/replace/remove pass remains separate, but every
current choice must already survive the named operative relationship, explicit
fit classification, gameplay tradeoff, and non-aggregate test.

Soul Relics received the deepest restoration: the card now preserves their
outside-host root/Charm topology, independently paid cross-source weaving,
bearer/allied ownership, nonrecursive evidence, non-gating Soul resonance,
qualitative instance variation, prism/affinity/archetype structure, bounded
dual results, persistent artifact lineage, visible insufficiency/break/renew/
rearm, integrated accounts, collection/duplicate behavior, and named
degenerations. These remain direction-selected worksheet inputs, not a stealth
EP-D02 amendment.

**Prerequisite correction, 2026-09-24:** the earlier pointer to one combined
`RCS-03` transformation/authority card was not prerequisite-ready. The source
instructions at C131/C132 require the mutable semantic domain, Relic-side
grammar/state binding, and contingent `H`/`S` sampling to be resolved separately
before cause and agency; the old `RCS-04` also bundled collision precedence with
committed-output identity. The non-counting parent aliases are therefore split
into `RCS-03A`–`RCS-03E` and `RCS-04A`–`RCS-04B`. The owner selected
`RCS-03A-C`: state-supporting Relics may transform remembered condition and
every boundary-supporting Relic may transform remembered attunement, without a
same-definition state/boundary cross-product.

**Second prerequisite correction, 2026-09-24:** the first split still
overcompressed `RCS-03B`. It assumed a cross-cut pending evaluation existed
while combining its lifetime topology, initiation, Relic-side bind cut, later
legal context-change disposition, participant cancellation, automatic expiry/
horizon, and concurrency. The owner selected `RCS-03B1-A`, cut-atomic
evaluation, so `RCS-03B2`–`RCS-03B7` are pruned. **Third prerequisite
correction, 2026-09-24:** the next audit found that `RCS-03C` still combined
eligible-cut cadence, invocation authority, and contingent evidence
acquisition. The thrice-amended register replaces it with
`RCS-03C1`–`RCS-03C3`; the owner selected `RCS-03C1-A`, immediate eligibility.
**Fourth prerequisite correction, 2026-09-24:** `RCS-03C2` still combined
invocation-control source with live-versus-precommitted participant authority,
so the register replaces it with `RCS-03C2A`–`RCS-03C2B`. The sole active owner
card is `RCS-03C2A` under `SR-04`. Do not revive C171 as an owner question.
Exact Relic tuple atomicity is derived, not a choice.

**Agent correction:** the first internal version of that second amendment
still omitted initiation, participant cancellation, and automatic expiry even
while the card named their gameplay consequences. They are independent
conditional slots, not implementation details, and were restored before owner
presentation.

**Agent correction:** I initially suspected the unusually large tracked diffs
might be line-ending conversion. A direct HEAD/worktree comparison disproved
that: HEAD contains the much older short documents, while the worktree contains
the accumulated design work. No EOL normalization was performed, and no file
was reverted on that mistaken premise.

## 2026-09-15 standalone-game north star — Souls and Simulacra

The owner has named the intended standalone game **Souls and Simulacra** and
requires every remaining choice to remain consistent with that identity. The
standing design ideal is *Achintya Bheda Abheda* (*acintya-bhedābheda*): when a
mechanic represents a relationship among Soul, simulacrum, Relic, Charm,
combatant, source, or result, prefer meaningful continuity or participation
and real, mechanically legible distinction coexisting at the same operative
boundary. Separate states, keys, definitions, or catalog entries respectively
supplying “unity” and “difference,” mere co-presence, labels, or cosmetic
variation do not by themselves satisfy the ideal. This is an inspirational
game-design analogy, not theological equivalence, a silent universal invariant,
or an automatic mechanic selector. It supplies no unstated rule and reopens no
selected direction. Every future recommendation must name the operative
relationship/boundary, rate the fit as direct, partial, neutral, or aggregate,
and state the gameplay tradeoff; surface any tension instead of hiding it.
Never present an incompatible option as though it satisfies the ideal. If no
offered direction can remain consistent, add or recommend an explicit
replacement rather than weakening the north star.

Complete the current full-assurance design-framework pass against the existing
SS2-derived skeleton first; then begin a separate expansion, audit, and
adaptation pass over the already-designed engine. Do not begin implementation.
Existing vanilla evidence, capture provenance, and the byte-identical
measurement oracle remain in their current SS2 lane and are not renamed or
reinterpreted by this direction.

recommended), non-quantity-bearing definition entitlements (B), or definitions
statically typed between the two representations (C). This does not inherit the
ordinary equipment state machine or decide acquisition, duplicate events,
active/simultaneous use, transfer, durability, Charm representation, or release.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.7-A: every available Soul Relic candidate
requires one uniquely identified instance record while it exists. If later
rules permit two identical definitions, they remain separate IDs. This does
not permit duplicate acquisition, simultaneous operation, transfer, loss, or
evolution and does not inherit the ordinary-equipment item-state machine.

At that stage, EP-D02-C3c.8 became the sole active choice and asked what one
combatant-specific Charm collection holds, uniformly for input-type and
payoff-type Charms: uniquely identified instances (A, recommended), fungible
integer quantities per definition (B), or boolean definition membership (C).
Possession is not activation; duplicate awards, active count, simultaneous use,
attachment, consumption, rolls, transfer, and release remain open.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.8-A: every possessed input-type or
payoff-type Charm has an authoritative unique instance ID, and mechanically
identical Charms never collapse into a quantity or boolean collection entry.
This leaves room for per-item provenance, rolls, history, or evolution without
selecting any of them. Duplicate acquisition, active coexistence, attachment,
consumption, transfer, and loss remain open.

The first C3c.9 card then asked only whether zero active
Soul Relics is legal: everywhere (A, recommended), only outside at least one
disclosed optional Relic-attuned activity (B), or nowhere in combat once the
Relic ruleset is enabled (C). This fixes the active-set lower bound, not its
maximum. Zero roots means zero Charms and no weave; it does not suppress Bound
Soul or ordinary legal build sources.

The owner correctly challenged that legality-only card: a meaningful Relic
normally adds value, so “optional” does not imply equivalent power. The first
C3c.9 alternatives and recommendation are withdrawn before selection. Revised
C3c.9 asks the zero-root state's product status: legal omission with no parity
promise (A), a separately supported mutually exclusive no-Relic archetype (B),
or a mandatory personal Relic after a safe late enablement transition (C,
recommended). C cannot enable until later acquisition/loss/migration rules
guarantee a legal personal instance and non-stranding recovery; that guard does
not select the milestone, item, rate, or release. Maximum roots and exact budget
remain later.

The owner's `C` selects revised EP-D02-C3c.9-C. Before a later-defined
Relic-readiness transition, zero roots remains legal. Once the non-optional
enabled state begins, every combat using that ruleset requires at least one
active personal Relic and the combatant cannot opt back out. Enablement is
invalid until later acquisition, loss, and migration rules guarantee a legal
personal instance and non-stranding recovery. This requires neither an active
Charm nor a currently satisfiable weave and changes no authoritative wording.

The owner's `lets go with A` selects EP-D02-C3c.10-A: after safe enablement,
exactly one personal Soul Relic instance is active. A proposed second instance
fails the family-count validator even if it has a different definition, and
inactive owned Relics supply no grammar, resonance, payoff, or Charm authority.
The choice preserves many owned alternatives while making one root the focal
build thesis. It also makes simultaneous same-definition duplicate activation
structurally illegal, so that planned dependent question collapses rather than
receiving a vacuous card. Same-definition possession, duplicate acquisition,
Charm attachment/count, budget, and horizon remain later. This is a direction,
not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative
wording.

The non-normative [system-fit visual](docs/design/endless-build-system-map.svg)
now marks the one-root result as selected. It does not select a Relic
screen/host, Charm attachment/count/coexistence, source family, or budget.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.11-A: a legal combatant collection contains
at most one Soul Relic instance for each stable `relicDefinitionId`. An
operation that would create a second same-definition holding cannot commit that
illegal result. The one permitted holding remains a discrete instance under
C3c.7-A, but the selection creates no rolls, evolution, salvage, transfer,
loss, or duplicate-award outcome. This is a direction, not a complete accepted
EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.12-A: when an otherwise-earned reward selects
a Relic definition already in the receiving combatant's collection, and its
declared reward pool contains an otherwise-eligible unowned definition, the
committed Relic must be one of those unowned definitions. The event creates no
infusion receipt or Relic currency. If the pool is complete, no Relic item
commits and a later terminal-collection fallback must preserve the reward's
value. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and
changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex correction, 2026-09-12:** the first C3c.13 draft bundled final selector
authority with complete-versus-sampled candidate breadth, a fixed three-offer
count, weighted sampling, and a reachable pool-size requirement. A read-only
audit rejected it before owner selection; it is withdrawn and chose nothing.

The owner's `B` selects corrected EP-D02-C3c.13-B: whenever a committed
duplicate-substitute offer presents at least two candidates, the receiving
combatant's player selects exactly one and the system cannot override that
choice. One candidate resolves automatically. The complete design must contain
at least one reachable two-candidate event so the selected authority is real.
Candidate breadth, construction, ordering, and weighting remain open. This is a
direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.14-B: every duplicate-substitute offer uses
one uniform bounded subset. One later-selected fixed `offerSize >= 2` applies
to all such offers; when fewer candidates remain all are shown, and when more
remain exactly that many distinct definitions are shown under a later sampling
rule. At least one reachable pool must exceed the selected size or the choice
collapses to the rejected complete-pool direction. This is a direction, not a
complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.15-B: every duplicate-substitute offer
contains exactly three distinct eligible-unowned definitions when at least
three remain. With two, both are presented; with one, it resolves
automatically. At least one reachable declared pool must contain four or more
eligible-unowned definitions so the selected bounded offer differs materially
from complete-pool choice. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02
replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex correction, 2026-09-12:** the first C3c.16 draft treated personal
presentation-history adjustment as a peer to source-weighted and uniform
baselines. A read-only audit rejected that coupling before owner selection:
history protection could modify either baseline and creates separate state,
scope, cap, and reset choices. The draft is withdrawn and selected nothing.

The owner's `A` selects corrected EP-D02-C3c.16-A: every reward source declares
positive versioned Relic-definition weights, and the three-candidate substitute
set preserves them after conditioning on current eligibility and unowned
status. At least one reachable source must use materially nonuniform weights or
the direction collapses to uniform sampling. Exact weights, subset algorithm,
PRNG/seed, and order remain open. This is a direction, not a complete accepted
EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

The owner's `A` selects corrected EP-D02-C3c.17-A: every duplicate-substitute
offer uses the source-weighted baseline conditioned only on the current
eligible-unowned pool. Prior presentations and declines never alter later
candidate likelihood, and no sampling-relevant presentation-history state is
created. Numeric weights/algorithm, PRNG/seed, and order remain open. This is a
direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.18-A: every Relic-granting source declares a
versioned, nonempty terminal fallback through its existing non-Relic reward
economy. A complete-pool Relic event atomically resolves through that same
source-local fallback and creates no Relic item, mastery receipt, or dedicated
Relic currency. Exact contents and value equivalence remain open. This is a
direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.19-B: after membership is fixed, every
permutation of the displayed definitions has equal probability. The realized
order commits atomically with the offer and survives reload, reconnect, and
replay of that settlement. It never changes membership, sampling probability,
or selected definition. Exact shuffle algorithm, PRNG, and seed remain open.
This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.20-A: every created Soul Relic has exactly
one stable current `ownerCombatantId`, including while it waits in personal
claim escrow. No campaign, member, controller, seat, or profile owns it. This
does not make title permanent or permit a transfer. This is a direction, not a
complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.21-A: only the owning combatant's persistent
`custodianMemberId` may approve the owner side of a later-permitted
discretionary Relic mutation. Seat, controller, host, teammate, and AI status
grant no authority; an unavailable custodian leaves the choice pending absent a
later recovery rule. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02
replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.22-A: a Soul Relic's
`ownerCombatantId` never changes through gifting, trade, lending, campaign
storage, inheritance, or another voluntary player action. Recovery/migration
may only restore its intended existing owner. Transfer consent, exchange, and
horizon branches collapse. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02
replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex correction, 2026-09-12:** after recording C3c.23-A, I initially left
this paragraph in contradictory present tense. Before the owner's answer,
EP-D02-C3c.23 asked whether ordinary gameplay could cause no persistent loss
(A, recommended), reversible damage/dormancy (B), or permanent destruction
with an atomic legal replacement (C). At that point battle-local suppression,
explicit retirement/deletion, Charm loss, acquisition/attribution,
configuration horizon, persistence/migration, and release remained later. The
answer and derived Relic-lane correction immediately below supersede that
former frontier; there is no remaining Relic-host owner fork.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.23-A: ordinary gameplay can never
persistently impair, consume, confiscate, salvage, or destroy an owned Soul
Relic. Relics have no persistent charges, wear, damage, repair, or breakage
state. Battle-local suppression and explicit retirement/deletion remain
separate. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and
changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex correction, 2026-09-12:** the queue's next “Relic
host/multiplicity” entry would have been a fake owner choice. The dedicated
non-item surface, personal collection, mandatory post-enablement lower bound,
and maximum-one active rule already derive one logical personal Relic
activation lane: zero or one before safe enablement and exactly one afterward.
This does not choose a UI screen, serialized field, budget, Bound Soul host, or
Charm socket. The eventual replacement replay must state the derived lane.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.24 asked for a maximum of one Charm (A),
two with at least one real pair (B, recommended), or no family-specific cap
with a later finite limiter and real three-Charm build (C). The owner's `B`
selects a maximum of two: zero, one, or two active Charm instances are legal on
family count, a third is rejected, and at least one materially functional pair
must exist. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement,
and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.25 asked whether all type multisets were
legal (A), only one input plus one payoff (B, recommended), or only same-type
pairs (C). The owner's `B` selects the complementary pair: zero or one Charm
may be either type, while two must be exactly one input and one payoff. At least
one materially functional cross-type pair must exist. Homogeneous and therefore
same-definition co-activation is illegal; duplicate possession/acquisition
remains open. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement,
and changes no authoritative wording.

**Derived consequence, 2026-09-12:** the dependency on one root, maximum two,
and complementary composition leave one optional input lane and one optional
payoff lane, both modifying the sole active Relic. Shared versus split UI is
presentation; per-Relic saved sockets are persistence/horizon. Neither is a new
functional attachment/group owner choice.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.26 asked whether fixed lanes were
complete accounting (A, recommended), every active hierarchy reserved scarce
budget including the root-only state (B), or an authored nonempty proper subset
did so while functional free and paying configurations both existed (C). Any
budget had to bind at least one reachable proposal.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.26-A: the fixed one-root, optional input,
and optional payoff lanes are the Relic hierarchy's complete standing
accounting. Neither root nor active Charm reserves personal Rule Capacity or a
new fungible pool. Other legality, authored burdens, and compatibility remain.
The all-sixteen-Legendary endpoint is unchanged, and the charged-unit, pool,
tariff, and budget-progression branches collapse. This is a direction, not a
complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.27 asked whether actual Bound Soul
behavior could satisfy one position in C3c.2-A's source minimum: uniformly (A),
never (B, recommended), or under a nonempty proper pair-key subset (C).

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.27-B: Bound Soul behavior, state, and events
never satisfy a required Relic-weaver source position. The Bound-Soul/Relic
pair still selects its qualitative grammar variant, but at least two later-
eligible non-Bound-Soul sources must satisfy it. This selects no other source
family, atomization, multiplicity, accounting, rebinding, relationship, payoff,
or magnitude. It is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement,
and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.28 asked whether otherwise-eligible
combatant sources could originate from the bearer only (A), bearer or allies
(B, recommended), or any participating combatant including opponents (C).

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.28-B: bearer- and participating-ally-origin
sources may qualify; opponent origins do not. The completed system must contain
a functional all-bearer weave and a functional ally-origin weave. Each source
retains its origin and its own applicable ownership, accounting, payment,
declaration, execution, and controller rules; the Relic only evaluates its
canonical record. This does not decide exact bearer participation, source reuse,
families, atomization, arity, grammar, or payoff. It is a direction, not a
complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.29 asked whether every final effective
input-grammar entry required at least one bearer-origin source (A,
recommended), none had that minimum while a real zero-bearer all-ally weave
existed (B), or the portfolio contained materially functional required and
optional entries (C).

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.29-A: every successful effective input-
grammar entry requires at least one bearer-origin source. An otherwise-valid
zero-bearer relation fails at that origin-composition gate, and the functional
cooperative witness selected through C3c.28-B must combine a bearer source and
a genuine ally source. The requirement is versioned before combat and cannot
vary by route, format, opponent, runtime state, or results. The zero-bearer and
mixed-classifier branches collapse. This is a direction, not a complete
accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.30 asked which semantic dimensions the
Relic's combatant-source grammar addressed: action semantics only (A),
configured-build provenance only (B), or materially both (C, recommended).

The owner's `C` selects EP-D02-C3c.30-C: under the later composition rule, the
completed catalog must demonstrate materially functional action-semantic and
build-provenance sensitivity through reachable isolation counterfactuals. This
does not decide whether those dimensions appear in separate entries, together,
or both. A Technique's candidate action and build views do not become distinct
source atoms and cannot automatically fill two positions. This is a direction,
not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative
wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.31 classified final effective entries
materially sensitive to at least one selected basis (`E+`) by whether hybrid
action-and-build-sensitive entries were absent (A, recommended), universal
within `E+` (B), or coexisted materially with single-basis entries (C).

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.31-A: every basis-sensitive final effective
entry is either pure action or pure build; none is hybrid. The completed catalog
must contain reachable, materially functional, bearer-compliant entries of both
pure types with their isolation counterfactuals. Basis-neutral entry admission
remains separate. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02
replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.32 asked whether counted positions in
basis-sensitive entries (`P+`) were configuration-sufficient (A), uniformly
required contingent post-entry combat evidence (B, recommended), or materially
supported both evidence modes (C).

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.32-B: every `P+` position requires at least
one material semantic fact causally produced or changed by contingent legal
post-entry combat history. Frozen configuration, unavoidable deterministic
initialization, and delayed serialized copies of either are never sufficient;
reachable same-configuration histories must prove the evidence-gate difference.
Configuration may remain a legality condition or retained provenance. This is
a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.33 asked whether every counted position
needed an independently isolatable combat-evidence path (A, recommended), every
position was coupled-only (B), or both attribution classes were materially
supported (C).

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.33-A: every `P+` position must have at least
one reachable legal pair isolating a nonempty change within either the
occurrence-history projection or the current-state projection while the
opposite projection and outside inputs remain fixed. No isolation among fields
inside the varied projection is required, so this selects no atom boundary. A
position whose sole material path is inseparable `H`+`S` evidence is invalid,
and incomplete
analysis remains unresolved rather than proving coupling or invariance.
Additional coupled boundaries inside an otherwise independently attributable
position remain open. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02
replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.34 asked whether the completed catalog's
**independently isolatable** support set was occurrence history only (A),
current state only (B), or materially both (C, recommended).

The owner's `C` selects EP-D02-C3c.34-C: the completed basis-sensitive catalog
must contain at least one reachable, materially functional, bearer-compliant
`I_H` witness and at least one such `I_S` witness. Every `P+` position still
needs at least one independent path. One position may supply both forms only
through two distinct projection-isolation pairs. This does not decide whether
positions are independently single-form or dual-sensitive, their entry/Relic
grouping, their action/build mapping, additional coupled boundaries, or source
atoms/double counting. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02
replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.35 asked whether the independently dual-
sensitive subset of `P+` was empty (A, recommended), all of `P+` (B), or a
nonempty proper subset (C).

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.35-A: every counted position in `P+` is
independently history-only or independently state-only, never both. C3c.34-C
therefore forces nonempty support for both types through distinct positions in
reachable, materially functional, bearer-compliant final entries. This does not
decide whether the types share an entry or Relic, their action/build or
bearer/ally mapping, atoms or reuse, or additional coupled-boundary permission.
This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.36 asked whether every basis-sensitive
entry was independently history- or state-homogeneous (A), every such entry
contained distinct history-only and state-only positions (B), or the catalog
materially supported both homogeneous and cross-form entries (C, recommended).

The owner's `C` selects EP-D02-C3c.36-C: `E_X` is a nonempty proper subset of
`E+`. The completed catalog must contain a reachable, materially functional,
bearer-compliant cross-form entry with distinct history-only and state-only
positions plus a distinct functional homogeneous entry. Each position remains
independently single-form. This does not decide which homogeneous subtype
exists, whether entries share a Relic, or any operator, timing, arity, atom,
reuse, origin, action/build, identity, or extra-coupling rule. This is a
direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.37 asked whether the nonempty homogeneous
complement supported history-homogeneous entries only (A), state-homogeneous
entries only (B), or both types (C, recommended).

The owner's `C` selects EP-D02-C3c.37-C: distinct reachable, materially
functional, bearer-compliant history-homogeneous and state-homogeneous entries
are required in addition to the already-required cross-form entry. This derives
at least three distinct final effective entries but not three Relic definitions
and makes no action/build mapping. Each position remains independently single-
form. Stable identities, sources/atoms, operators, timing, arity, origin
mapping, payoff, and extra coupled boundaries remain open. This is a direction,
not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative
wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.38 asked whether none, all, or some of the
history-homogeneous, state-homogeneous, and cross-form temporal types must have
both a pure-action and a distinct pure-build entry.

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.38-B: all three temporal types are basis-
dual. The completed catalog therefore requires six distinct reachable,
materially functional, bearer-compliant final entries—one action and one build
witness for each temporal type—without requiring six Relic definitions, paired
variants, or pairwise-distinct mechanics, identities, or payoffs. Every entry
remains single-basis and every counted position independently single-form. The
temporal-to-basis support grid is complete, while its mapping to stable Relic,
resonance, and Charm identities remains open. This is a direction, not a
complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.39 asked whether zero, all, or some
supported Relic definitions could vary materially between instances inside one
fixed temporal-by-basis cell. Its first presentation recommended universal
definition stability. After the owner asked whether selected variable artifacts
could add gameplay rather than detract, the recommendation changed openly to a
mixed model whose exceptional class must later earn its complexity through
visible qualitative build puzzles rather than hidden superior/inferior rolls.

The owner's `Lets go with that` selects EP-D02-C3c.39-C: the completed catalog
must contain at least one materially participating definition that is invariant
under every legal same-definition instance substitution within every cell in
which it participates and at least one distinct definition with a reachable
material fixed-cell same-combatant counterfactual witness. The alternate
instances never coexist. This chooses no ratio, names, generation, disclosure,
selection, re-attunement, value, or balance rule; those—including the owner's
desired safeguards—remain later atomic choices. Cross-cell and payoff variation
also remain open. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02
replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex correction, 2026-09-13:** the first C3c.40 draft asked about input-
Charm instance sensitivity while its `E+` participant set could still be
empty. Its A bundled empty and nonempty-stable states, while B/C silently
selected participation. An intermediate audit-only domain-support draft would
then have prematurely decided whether neutral Charm use exists on the likely
branch. Both were withdrawn before owner presentation and selected nothing.

Before the owner's answer, EP-D02-C3c.40 asked how broadly the nonempty set `I`
of materially functional input-Charm definitions must participate in the six-
cell `E+` domain: none, all, or a nonempty proper subset.

The owner's `B` selects EP-D02-C3c.40-B: `I_E=I`. Every functional input-Charm
definition must have at least one legal instance with a reachable, material,
bearer-compliant use in at least one of the six selected cells. Its active-
instance versus empty-input-lane counterfactual must change the final predicate
result in otherwise-identical legal worlds. This is existential per definition,
not a requirement on every instance, every use, or every cell. It excludes an
input-Charm definition whose only material uses are basis-neutral, but neither
requires nor forbids a participating definition from also supporting `E0`.
Exact cell assignments, neutral admission/content, and stable mapping remain
open. This is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and
changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex example correction, 2026-09-13:** the first C3c.40-B illustration mixed
action names with supplying-component provenance and could read as a forbidden
hybrid entry. It is replaced by a pure-action history relation. The owner
selected participation breadth, not that example; no direction changed.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.41-A: `V_I` is empty. Every materially
functional input-Charm definition must pass complete same-definition/version
invariance inside every `E+` temporal-by-basis cell in which it participates.
Two legally comparable instances cannot return different final-predicate
Booleans on the same reachable canonical valuation while the exact Soul Relic
instance/state, Bound Soul, payoff Charm, other configuration facts,
evaluation boundary, version, and cell remain fixed. Incomplete analysis is
unresolved. A definition with fewer than two legally comparable instances has
no nontrivial comparison, and A promises no obtainable interchangeable copies.
Unique IDs retain ownership, custody, provenance, and nonmechanical-history
roles. Cross-cell behavior, `E0`, same-instance evolution, different Relic or
resonance contexts, and payoff behavior remain open. This is a direction, not
a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex dependency correction, 2026-09-13:** the earlier queue put base-
grammar ownership next. Re-derivation showed that a Relic-owned base followed
by an unrestricted resonance transform and a pair-owned grammar can encode the
same combat behavior. C3c.3-B also guarantees only two distinct variants
somewhere in the pair map, so it does not yet force Bound Soul identity to
matter while one Relic is fixed. EP-D02-C3c.42 therefore asks the material
gameplay prerequisite first; base ownership remains later and must eventually
carry a real behavioral or durable-lineage invariant rather than a storage
label.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.42-A: `D_S=D+`. Every supported Soul Relic
definition must have at least one exact instance/state and fixed Charm context
where two supported Bound Souls produce different cell-tagged extensional
final-input portfolios solely because Soul identity differs. Both portfolios
are built before Soul-local reachability filtering and compared over a
nonempty common reachable valuation envelope; the distinguishing behavior's
material bearer-compliant cell witness must occur inside that envelope, so a
Bound Soul's independent combat behavior cannot fake resonance. This derives
at least two supported Souls but does not require every Relic instance, Soul
pair, Charm context, entry, or cell to differ or establish a power advantage.
It is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and changes no
authoritative wording.

**Codex correction, 2026-09-14:** the first C3c.43 draft asked about
simultaneous final `E+` input-channel multiplicity. Several internal result
slots can collapse to one material aggregate such as `P or Q`; preventing that
collapse required silently selecting player-disclosed recipe identities and
separately observable progress/results. Because that communication/consumer
rule has not had its own owner choice, the draft was withdrawn before owner
presentation and selected nothing. Multiplicity returns after that
prerequisite.

The owner's `A` selects EP-D02-C3c.43-A: `U=D+`. Every legal, reachable,
versioned, materially `E+`-operative tuple of exact Soul Relic instance, input-
affecting state, and later-authorized grammar-evaluation boundary must have at
least one complete C3c.42 same-Relic Bound-Soul witness. The Soul pair, Charm
context, cell, and predicate may differ by realization. This preserves
C3c.39-C's per-copy qualitative variation but prevents any materially usable
version from becoming wholly Soul-indifferent. It does not make every legal
record operative, require every Soul pair or context to differ, promise Charm-
free resonance or equal power, authorize evolution, or select an evaluation
horizon. It is a direction, not a complete accepted EP-D02 replacement, and
changes no authoritative wording.

**Codex frontier correction, 2026-09-14:** player visibility is sufficient but
not logically necessary to make two result states material; a downstream
mechanical consumer could distinguish them. The actual prerequisite is whether
the player-semantic result identity and the pre-payoff consumer identity align.
Payoff Charms are payoff-only, so their data and arbitration must be projected
out before this input-result partition; they may map existing classes to
consequences but cannot split or merge the input vocabulary.

**Owner decision, 2026-09-17:** EP-D02-C3c.96-A was selected.
`E^S_v=D^{U=}_{locked,v} x S_v`: every supported Soul changes assigned
noncore singleton map on some exact-key pair inside every union-locked
definition. The witnessing pair may differ by Soul. This closes local and

everyone or every pair to change everyone. Tight floors remain `6/9/54`;
Soul counts remain `{6,8,9,10,12,14,15}`, tight degree five is attained, and
degree six remains legal. Fixed-key fit is direct, each witnessed same-Soul
cross-key relation partial, and universal definition-by-Soul coverage
aggregate. This changes no authoritative wording.

Soul-changing pair. It gives each name one coherent, previewable whole-roster

C does not require every pair to change every Soul. It retains tight floors

All branches preserve Soul counts `{6,8,9,10,12,14,15}`. Each changed same-

**Codex C131 frontier result, 2026-09-20:** the next prerequisite-ready atom is

corresponding state is new. Circuit-local state counts as `P` if it crosses a

semantic class.

An occurrence-specific permission, reservation, queue, consumption,
suppression, or other authorization lineage remains recurrence control exactly
when changing it, with predicate facts and every other `X^-(chi)` input fixed,
can change whether this occurrence is complete. Clearing or renaming a field
cannot erase that meaning. Projection membership is nonexclusive: a coordinate
that both expresses relationship state and materially carries authorization
belongs to both, so changing it proves neither isolation. A genuine
relationship fact whose authorization force is exhausted remains predicate-
side even if an earlier recurrence difference helped produce it.

`I_P(w,d)` exists when a chosen-arm pair differs nontrivially in predicate
facts at `chi` while recurrence control and every other `q2` input are equal.
`I_R(w,d)` exists when recurrence control differs while every predicate fact
and other input are equal. A single pair changing both proves neither form; one
opportunity may prove both only through two distinct valid isolation pairs.
Earlier histories may differ beyond C140's anchored-suffix constraints, so this
is an extensional classification of the marginal decisive input, not proof of
mediator-path purity or sole historical causation.

not mean predicate-plus-recurrence. The selected A makes the set empty: every
legal path contains some independently attributable decisive-input comparison.

**Codex C151 frontier result, 2026-09-23:** same-decisive-cut dual-form

aligned completion direction: either the actual arm completes and both
counterfactuals do not, or the actual arm does not complete and both
counterfactuals do. One counterfactual cannot accelerate while the other delays
completion at that cut. Pair-specific counterfactual worlds remain separate.

neither form.

C164-A leaves only `{P}`, `{R}`, and `{P,R}` support at a joint opportunity,

A requires, for every exact legal `(k,w,d)` satisfying `J^{both-op}`, one

`pi_R in Lambda_R(w,d)` under the same `u(w,d)` and `O^-`. B requires, for

Accounts at different opportunities or histories cannot be stitched. A panel,

Every branch inherits C164-A's lawful human-control capability at every joint

**Owner decision, 2026-09-24:** EP-D02-C3c.165-A selected. Therefore
`U^{pre-all-op2}_v=D^{dual}_v`: every mechanical joint opportunity on every
legal history has a qualifying P pair and distinct qualifying R pair
intelligible to its one actual command-holder under the same exact authorized
`O^-` before commitment. At the exact `(k,w,d,u,O^-_u)` boundary, continuing
Soul–Relic context and both distinct forms coexist **directly**; their later
relationship to `q2`, effects, and payoff remains **partial**; excluding form-
hiding is **neutral/protective**; and universal opportunity/history/tag
saturation is **aggregate**. C165-A collapses support states to `{P,R}` without

adds no floor; singleton and opaque opportunity counts are zero, and every
fixed catalog and width floor remains unchanged. The authoritative decision
record remains unchanged.

**Codex C166 frontier result, 2026-09-24:** per-tag integrated two-form
semantic-account participation is the next Soul Relic information atom. For
an exact joint opportunity `(k,w,d)`, let `A^-_u(w,d)` be the complete set of
canonical, versioned player-semantic accounts authorized inside
`O^-_u(w,d)`. An account has one authored causal scope and provenance
independent of localization, layout, asset duplication, page breaks, or
rendering. One account may render through multiple explicitly linked views;
placing independent accounts in one page or panel does not merge them. A
neutral baseline may supply legal-arm identities and already-defined general
vocabulary but no target-specific P/R mapping.

Let `Lambda_F(w,d|xi)` contain qualifying form-`F` pairs made intelligible by
that baseline plus account `xi` alone. Define

`Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d)={(xi,pi_P,pi_R) :
xi in A^-_u(w,d), pi_P in Lambda_P(w,d|xi),
pi_R in Lambda_R(w,d|xi), pi_P != pi_R,
and xi explicitly presents those interventions as the distinct predicate and
recurrence faces of the same continuing tag and atomic choice}`,

`J^{pre-int}(w,d) iff J^{pre-both-op}(w,d)
and Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d) is nonempty`,

`W^{pre-int}_v(k)={w in W_v(k) : some d in D^{joint}(w)
has J^{pre-int}(w,d)}`,

and

`E^{pre-int}_v={k in D^{dual}_v : W^{pre-int}_v(k) is nonempty}`.

This is semantic integration rather than physical colocation. One versioned
account may render through tooltip, codex, audio, or linked views; one asset or
screen may contain separate accounts. C165-A guarantees that the whole `O^-`
makes both forms legible at every joint opportunity, not that any one account
is jointly sufficient.

```text
INTEGRATED SEMANTIC ACCOUNT
“Converge restores the Covenant and opens its Echo to return.
 Compared with Invoke Only, bond restoration is the predicate face.
 Compared with Rekindle Only, return permission is the recurrence face.”

COMPOSITION-ONLY ACCOUNTS
Covenant primer: Converge vs Invoke Only explains P.
Echo-return primer: Converge vs Rekindle Only explains R.
Both are available before choice, but neither account explains their unity.
```

A, recommended, makes `E^{pre-int}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every dual tag has at least
one exact history and joint opportunity with one authorized semantic account
that explains a qualifying P pair and distinct R pair as two faces of the same
continuing Soul–Relic choice. At `(k,w,d,u,O^-_u,xi)`, relationship unity and
both distinctions coexist **directly**; their later relation to `q2`, effects,
and payoff is **partial**; rejecting fused or merely colocated pseudo-
integration is **neutral/protective**; and universal tag participation is
**aggregate**. Costs are synthesis, localization, and validation per tag,
denser explanations, repetitive “two faces” boilerplate, less interpretive
mystery, and token compliance through one obscure integrated account per tag.
A does not require every history or opportunity, one reference asset, or one
screen.

B makes `E^{pre-int}_v` empty. C165-A still makes both forms intelligible at
every joint choice, but no account simultaneously supplies both exact form
accounts and explicitly relates them as two faces. A jointly sufficient
account with unrelated clauses, or a relational overview lacking both exact
pair mappings, remains integration-negative. Separate concise bond/predicate
and recurrence/permission modules are the strongest countercase: they preserve
reusable teaching and reduce dense-explanation, maintenance, and localization
coupling. Each form is **direct** in its own module; cross-account unity is
**partial**; explicit integration is **neutral/unsupported**; modularity may be
**neutral/protective**; and universal integration-negative topology is
**aggregate**. Costs are split attention and memory, weaker thematic synthesis,
apparent fragmentation, and reliance on player inference.

C makes `E^{pre-int}_v` nonempty proper. Integrated and integration-negative
dual tags coexist. Local cases inherit A/B's ratings and coexistence is
**aggregate**. Costs are another information dialect, unequal learning burden,
clarity or interpretive-flexibility premium risk, and token class membership.
Example: Ashen has an integrated Convergence account; Dreamglass exposes
Facet and Echo accounts at every choice but no account relates them.

A/B/C are exhaustive because `E^{pre-int}_v` is a subset of the nonempty dual
domain: whole, empty, or nonempty proper. A separate catalog-existence card is
unnecessary because B covers global absence and C covers nonempty nonuniversal
participation. C165-A prevents a negative tag from hiding either form;
negative means only that no integrated semantic account exists. Same-reference
and same-screen states create no fourth class because they remain orthogonal
later axes.

A requires one exact `(w,d,u,O^-_u,xi,pi_P,pi_R)` witness per dual tag. B
requires exhaustive analysis of every dual tag, legal history, joint opportunity,
qualifying P/R pair combination, and canonical authorized pre-choice account,
proving no account is jointly sufficient and integrating. C combines one
positive tag with one exhaustively integration-negative tag. Failure to find a
shared tooltip, reference, or panel is not negative proof; incomplete account
or content enumeration remains unresolved.

Same-`O^-` co-legibility is not integration. A shared `referenceId`, asset,
page, menu, heading, or simultaneous screen is insufficient without one
canonical account's cross-form semantic relation. A generic “does both,”
visual adjacency, icon/color similarity, two links under one heading, or
player inference cannot manufacture an integrated witness. Different
renderings do not split one versioned account; arbitrary concatenation does
not create one. Generated text counts only when its canonical grammar emits a
truthful integrated account for the target arms. One bundled pair cannot
satisfy both forms, and aliases count once.

No cross-opportunity, history, tag, role, seat, version, or post-commit
stitching is allowed. Guides, private AI state, post-choice feedback, data
mining, and uncommunicated other-seat information remain invalid. A stable
generic account may support multiple tags only when truthful for each exact
arm set; those are separate indexed witnesses.

Every branch inherits C165-A's same-role two-form co-legibility and C164-A's
lawfully human-controllable observation contract. C166 adds no controller
count or identity and does not require actual human occupancy. B/C cannot
distribute form-legibility evidence across players; both forms remain within
the same command-holder's `O^-`, and only their semantic provenance differs.

**Owner decision, 2026-09-24:** EP-D02-C3c.166-A selected. Therefore
`E^{pre-int}_v=D^{dual}_v`: every mechanically dual tag has at least one exact
legal history and joint opportunity with one canonical authorized semantic
account that makes a qualifying P pair and distinct R pair intelligible and
explicitly relates them as two faces of the same continuing Soul–Relic choice.
At `(k,w,d,u,O^-_u,xi)`, unity and both real distinctions coexist
**directly**; their later relation to `q2`, effects, and payoff remains

C166-A requires neither integration on every history or opportunity, reuse of
one account or reference, nor one screen. It raises no inherited catalog or

opportunity, two form memberships, two legible pairs, two mechanical pairs,
and three continuations otherwise remain sufficient. The authoritative

**Codex C167 frontier result, 2026-09-24:** per-history integrated-account
saturation is the next Soul Relic information atom. Retain
`W^{pre-int}_v(k)` and define

`W^{pre-int0}_v(k)=W_v(k) setminus W^{pre-int}_v(k)`

and

`U^{pre-all-int}_v={k in D^{dual}_v :
W^{pre-int}_v(k)=W_v(k)}`.

A history in `W^{pre-int0}_v(k)` is integration-negative: every exact joint
opportunity on it has `Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d)` empty. C165-A still makes
distinct qualifying P and R pairs intelligible to the same actual command-
holder at every such choice. Negative means only that no one canonical account
at a target choice both supplies the exact mappings and explicitly relates
them as two faces. Separate complete modules, one jointly sufficient account
with unrelated clauses, and a relational overview missing an exact pair
mapping remain negative.

A, recommended, makes `U^{pre-all-int}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every exact legal history
of every dual tag contains at least one joint opportunity with an integrated
account. Account identity, opportunity, pairs, wording, and rendering may vary
between histories; other opportunities may remain negative. Each account
boundary is **direct**; continuity across distinct histories is **partial**;
excluding wholly integration-negative histories is **neutral/protective**;
and universal history/tag saturation is **aggregate**. A closes C166-A's
obscure-history loophole while preserving modular teaching elsewhere. Costs
are history-wide proof, synthesis/localization, repetition or contrived
accounts, less route-specific mystery, and one-buried-account token
compliance.

Example: Ashen's Mercy and Defiance histories each contain some Convergence
choice whose one account maps the exact P and R comparisons and explains their
unity. The two histories may use different canonical accounts and views.

B makes `U^{pre-all-int}_v` empty. Every dual tag has C166-A's integrated-
positive history and a distinct integration-negative history. Its strongest
case is biography-sensitive initiation: one route explicitly names the unity;
another supplies complete modular truths and lets lived play or interpretation
synthesize them. Positive accounts remain **direct**; both form comparisons on
the negative history remain individually **direct** within the same `O^-`
while explicit integration is **neutral/unsupported**; same-tag continuity
across histories is **partial**;
deliberate mystery may be **neutral/protective**; and universal mixing is
**aggregate**. Costs are route-dependent comprehension, guide advantage,
apparent inconsistency, and token positive or negative histories.

Example: Ashen's Mercy history carries the integrated Convergence account. On
its Defiance history, every joint choice still explains both forms, but
complete analysis finds only separate modules, unrelated joint clauses, or
incomplete relational overviews.

C makes `U^{pre-all-int}_v` a nonempty proper subset. History-saturated and
history-mixed dual tags coexist. Local cases inherit A/B's ratings and catalog
coexistence is **aggregate**. Costs are another information dialect, uneven
learning burden, clarity or interpretive-flexibility premiums, saturation and
negative-proof burdens, and token class membership. Example: Ashen integrates
on every history; Dreamglass has one integrated Study history and one Facet
history whose forms are fully legible but never integrated.

A/B/C are exhaustive after C166-A. Every tag's positive-history set is
nonempty, so a tag is saturated or has both positive and negative histories;
the saturated subset is whole, empty, or nonempty proper. A never-integrated

A requires one exact integrated witness on every legal `(k,w)`. B requires,

asset, page, screen, heading, and simultaneous-rendering identity remain
neither necessary nor sufficient.

Every branch inherits same-role two-form co-legibility and a lawfully human-
controllable observation contract at every joint opportunity. A negative

players, or rely on AI-private information. C167 adds no controller identity

**Codex C170 frontier result, 2026-09-24:** because C166's `xi` is a canonical,
versioned semantic account, equality below follows authored causal scope and
provenance rather than raw ID, wording, reference asset, layout, or rendering.
Define

`I^{pre-int}_v(k,w,d)={xi : there exist pi_P,pi_R with
(xi,pi_P,pi_R) in Gamma^{pre-int}_v(w,d)}`,

`P^{pre-reuse}_v(k,w)={{d_i,d_j} subseteq D^{joint}(w) :
d_i != d_j and I^{pre-int}_v(k,w,d_i) intersect
I^{pre-int}_v(k,w,d_j) is nonempty}` for `w in W^{2j}_v(k)`,

`W^{pre-reuse}_v(k)={w in W^{2j}_v(k) :
P^{pre-reuse}_v(k,w) is nonempty}`,

and

`E^{pre-reuse}_v={k in D^{dual}_v :
W^{pre-reuse}_v(k) is nonempty}`.

The positive relation is one exact `(k,w,{d_i,d_j},xi)`: two distinct joint
choices on one legal history at which the same canonical account independently
satisfies `Gamma^{pre-int}`. Their exact P/R pairs may differ.

A, recommended, makes `E^{pre-reuse}_v=D^{dual}_v`. Every dual tag has one
exact history and one pair of distinct joint opportunities sharing a canonical
integrated-account identity; other histories and pairs may remain reuse-
negative. A learned relational grammar can return as the same covenant in a
new tactical expression. Local C168 integration remains **direct**. The same
account's participation across two commitment-separated choices is
**partial**, not a new same-boundary direct realization; lived recognition is
likewise **partial** because presentation sameness and actual notice are not
required; anti-aliasing is **neutral/protective**; and universal tag
participation is **aggregate**. Costs are repetition, generic-account pressure,
stale-context risk, coupled maintenance/versioning, and token compliance.
Example: one Ashen Mercy history's Tend and later Manifest choices both
authorize `Covenant–Echo v3`, which truthfully maps their potentially different
exact P/R comparisons.

B makes `E^{pre-reuse}_v` empty. Every pair of distinct joint opportunities on
every legal history of every dual tag has disjoint qualifying integrated-
account sets. C169-A makes this nonvacuous; accounts may still recur across
different histories or tags. Its strongest case is biography-specific
semantic evolution rather than one flattening catch-all account. Local unity
and distinction remain **direct**; same-tag/history mechanical continuity is
**partial**; same-account continuity is **neutral/unsupported**; protecting
genuine semantic change may be **neutral/protective**; and universal separation
is **aggregate**. Costs are relearning, content/validation burden, terminology
drift, weak recognizable continuity, and cosmetic-alias novelty. Example:
Tend uses `Covenant Under Ash v1`; Manifest uses `Echo at the Threshold v1`;
neither account qualifies at the other choice.

C makes `E^{pre-reuse}_v` nonempty proper. Reuse-capable and same-history
reuse-negative dual tags coexist. Local cases inherit A/B's ratings and class
coexistence is **aggregate**. Costs are another account-continuity dialect,
uneven learning/authoring burden, clarity or memorability premiums, exhaustive
negative proof, and token positive membership. Example: Ashen reuses
`Covenant–Echo v3`, while every Dreamglass multi-opportunity history uses
pairwise-disjoint qualifying accounts.

A/B/C are exhaustive. C169-A gives every dual tag a multi-opportunity history;
C168-A makes every joint opportunity's `I^{pre-int}` nonempty. A tag has a
same-history pair with a nonempty identity intersection or it does not;
`E^{pre-reuse}_v` is whole, empty, or nonempty proper.

A requires one exact `w,d_i,d_j,xi` per dual tag and a separate integrated
witness at each opportunity. B requires exhaustive history, opportunity-pair,
canonical-account, and qualifying-mapping enumeration proving every
intersection empty. C combines one positive and one exhaustively negative
distinct tag. Incomplete enumeration remains unresolved.

Same reference, page, string, icon, narrator, UI position, or equivalent prose
does not prove reuse; an alias cannot hide changed semantic scope or version.
The same account merely present at both choices does not count unless it
qualifies as integrated at both. P at one and R at another cannot be stitched.
Different histories, branch-exclusive choices, reloads, replays, player memory,
generic primers, and neutral baselines cannot manufacture an edge. Rendering
differences do not defeat reuse when one exact canonical identity remains
truthful and qualifying at both.

C170-A requires one edge only. `{a},{a},{b}` satisfies it without all-pairs
overlap. `{a,b},{b,c},{a,c}` has all-pairs overlap but no whole-history common
identity. C170 therefore does not select every-pair overlap, connectedness, or
one account common to the whole history.

| B | 3 | 1 | 2 | 2 | 0 / 1 | 2 | 4 / 4 | 4 / 6 |

dual tag has at least one exact legal history and one pair of distinct,
commitment-separated joint opportunities whose qualifying integrated-account
sets share one exact canonical identity. The same account independently
integrates both exact forms at both opportunities; its exact P/R mappings,
arms, references, wording, and rendering may differ. Other histories and
pairs may remain reuse-negative. C168's local relation remains **direct**,
same-account participation across the two temporal commitments is **partial**,
anti-aliasing is **neutral/protective**, and universal tag participation is
**aggregate**. C170-A raises no C169 mechanical floor. The authoritative
decision record remains unchanged.

**Closure-index correction, 2026-09-24:** EP-D02-C3c.171 remains unanswered,
but it is reclassified as `AUTHOR/EVALUATE`, not `OWNER`. It asks only whether
every dual tag's multi-opportunity histories contain some canonical
account-reuse edge, every dual tag mixes reuse-positive and reuse-negative
histories, or those tag classes coexist. Its own boundary changes no player
action, authoritative transition, local mechanic, information surface,
power/release result, or implementation obligation. Preserve the alternatives
as catalog/evaluation possibilities; none is selected. C170-A is sufficient
for framework closure. The proposed three-opportunity and graph descendants
are pruned unless later evidence passes the master closure index's reopening
test. **Corrected continuation:** the combined RCS-03 instruction was
overcompressed, and its first split still left the binding row bundled. The
owner selected `RCS-03A-C` and then `RCS-03B1-A`. Pending-only
`RCS-03B2`–`RCS-03B7` are pruned. A third prerequisite audit split the still-
bundled `RCS-03C` parent into eligible-cut cadence, invocation authority, and
evidence acquisition. The owner selected `RCS-03C1-A`, immediate eligibility.
A fourth audit split invocation-control source from participant-authorization
timing. Continue at registered Relic slot `RCS-03C2A` under `SR-04`;
conditional `RCS-03C2B`, `RCS-03C3`, cause, and agency follow separately.

R11 begins only
after R10 completes and first chooses package proximity for R10.7's selected
domain before dependent caps.
Lower Capacity bands remain regression coverage rather than full-48 evidence.
Migration/version failure authority, exact communication and taxonomy/support
authoring, and the complete normative replay follow. The authoritative decision
record is unchanged; EP-D01, EP-D02, and EP-D07 remain accepted, and
implementation remains blocked.

## 2026-09-04 Endless owner-decision update — EP-D02 and EP-D01 amendments

EP-D02 is closed through Zanzagar's explicit `revise — accepted replacement as
replayed`, and EP-D01 clauses 6–7 are closed through `revise — accepted
amendments as replayed`. The authoritative record transcribes the exact
eighteen-clause EP-D02 replacement and the two exact amended clauses.

Each persistent gladiator now owns personal Rule Capacity, starting at 0 and
rising through eighteen stable authored championship milestone lineages in six
`+2,+3,+3` chapters to a finite v1 ceiling of 48. All sixteen mapped item
positions may carry otherwise-legal Legendary payloads; categorical Load is
0/1/2/3, both equipped weapons reserve Load, and there is no maximum-one
3-Load-identity rule. Capacity cannot be pooled or loaned. Full-rank, Training
Assistance, and precommitted Recovery victories qualify every participating
persistent frozen-roster winner, with Recovery capacity as the sole exception
to that branch's otherwise zero mechanical grants. Receipts are personal,
bounded, atomic, and idempotent; lower routes clamp effective Capacity and
require explicit legal selection without mutating saved equipment.

At most one equipped Legendary may Ascend. Its item/Core lock for the Circuit;
after each encounter preview and before its first attempt, its owner may retain
or select one learned exact-lineage Branch, which then persists through defeat,
reload, and Rematches. Training Assistance and Recovery use the exact accepted
reselection boundaries. Team Tactic has one separate nonfungible team allowance.
Standalone Forms, Signature designations, and Keystones are removed as sources;
their concepts may be item-contained. Clean-boundary migration preserves every
item and an illegal old configuration until the owner explicitly replaces it.

The old four-unit EP-A03 Reconstruction Tray is superseded, not rescaled to 48;
EP-A03 requires redesign. EP-D04 also requires revised dominance wording that
permits a theoretical all-Legendary optimum while preserving useful mixed-rarity
matchups. EP-D03–EP-D06 and EP-A01–EP-A03 remain open. Implementation remains
blocked and is not authorized. Resume with the newest dated handoff and
`$ss2-progression-design`; the next coupled product frontier is EP-D04.

## 2026-09-03 Endless owner-decision update — EP-D07

EP-D07 is closed through Zanzagar's explicit `Accepted` response to the
complete replay. A persistent gladiator is mode-neutral across 1v1, 2v2, and
3v3; format and roster lock per Circuit without resetting Bound Soul, levels,
stats, equipment, Legendary Lineages, or personal Tactics. Every allied seat in
the first playable version requires a distinct connected human. Allied AI fill,
one-human multi-seat control, and AI takeover are excluded. A recognized
disconnect finishes an already-committed action exactly once and then pauses at
the next action boundary for same-seat reconnect or the established
team-abandonment path. A separately accepted corrected nine-clause dropout
supplement makes the versioned grace period visible and accepted at Circuit entry. Expiry
never resolves automatically: it activates only that absent member's narrow
conditional consent, every connected roster member must approve abandonment,
and any presence change makes the old proposal stale. A terminal committed
action settles without a timer, while a reconnect restores only that member and
cannot resume play until all required allies return. In 2v2 the one
remaining member may approve after the other's expiry; in 3v3 both remaining
members approve after one expiry, or the sole remaining member after two. No
connected member means no automatic result. Recovery retains its original
roster, custody, and human-seat admission.

The authoritative wording and its `[V]/[U]` boundary are in
[`docs/design/endless-progression-decisions.md`](docs/design/endless-progression-decisions.md#ep-d07--mode-neutral-gladiators-and-human-only-allied-mvp).
The acceptance makes a minimum genuine two-human session, durable pause,
authenticated reconnect, and implementation of the accepted grace/abandonment
protocol new playable blockers. The product liveness rule is no longer open,
but transport topology, authenticated presence, exact grace duration/storage,
and reconnect-versus-receipt serialization remain `[U]`. This does not remove
the generic resolver's AI/controller capabilities, promise later
companion/hot-seat modes, or authorize implementation.

The corrected supplement supersedes an eight-clause replay that I withdrew
before commit: it incorrectly said any reconnect resumed play despite another
possible 3v3 dropout and left “Recovery remains available” ambiguous about
reduced-roster entry. The owner explicitly accepted the corrected replay; the
withdrawn draft is not authoritative.

EP-D06 remains pending: the accepted EP-D07 replay did not include D06's
separate choice of deterministic 2v2 under designed rules, so that constraint
was not silently smuggled into this acceptance. EP-D03–EP-D06 and EP-A01–EP-A03
remain open; EP-D02 has since closed in the newer section above. Resume through
the newest dated handoff and the repo-local
`$ss2-progression-design` skill; do not reopen EP-D07 without an explicit
replacement decision.

## THE ARCHIVE LINE

Everything below this heading is frozen evidence and history. Read it to check
a claim; keep current state and corrections above this line.

## State at the end of the 2026-08-30 session

22 promoted goldens. 584 tests, all passing. 36 commits this session, across a
day of parallel work and one overnight run of twelve agents.

### Expected test profiles after PR #1

- A capture-bearing operator worktree with the complete ignored raw-trace
  archive runs all **584 tests: 584 passed, 0 skipped, 0 failed**.
- A fresh clone or worktree with none of those ignored traces runs **584 tests:
  583 passed, 1 skipped, 0 failed**. The skipped test is the raw-trace archive
  existence check; the committed observation and divergence integrity checks
  still run.
- A partial raw-trace archive does **not** skip: it fails and names every
  missing expected trace. This keeps the clean-clone accommodation from
  weakening evidence retention on an operator machine.

Read this section, then [`docs/overnight-agent-plan.md`](docs/overnight-agent-plan.md)
for how the parallel work is organised.

### What changed at the level of what this project can do

1. **Parallel capture works.** `-SaveDirectory` was never broken. Three
   concurrent sessions complete in 22s against ~45s serial, all matching
   promoted goldens, master save byte-identical.
2. **The leveled-gladiator arena route runs end to end.** A gladiator was taken
   1 → 4 and fought the tournament ladder to rank 2 in five of six attempts.
3. **The wrapper can stage a scenario and buy equipment**, both owner-approved,
   both declared in the evidence.
4. **The champion was decoded from the map before it was ever seen.** Reading
   `unleash_hell`'s hard-coded DNA through `initcharacter` and `battlevalues`
   PREDICTED `hitpointsmax` 110 and `armourclass` 86; twelve independent live
   draws recorded exactly those. Five `candidate-champion-*` fixtures exist.

### The single most important correction

**Five separate adversarial passes each found the same defect class — a test
whose assertion cannot fail — and each one had been hiding a real bug.** That is
now the project's most reliable signal, and the reason to keep running
write-nothing auditors against named claims.

Two forgeries against the promotion gate worked *by the documented pipeline*
and are now closed:

- **The launch-nonce gate was opt-out.** Copy a raw trace, change the ids,
  delete the nonce key: both ingest, both promote, and you get a golden claiming
  two independent sessions from one capture. Now mandatory for
  `injected-tape-runtime` on the same terms as `overdraw`.
- **An observation could carry unlimited invisible draws.** Cosmetic opcode
  rolls are excluded from matching by label regex on *both* sides, so a record
  with 120 fabricated debris rolls matched a 7-sample fixture. Records now
  refuse opcode samples outright — the doc's own reasoning (no instrumentation
  can observe the opcode stream) is exactly why no record should hold one.

**The 22 goldens are sound.** Independent re-derivation reproduced every one
byte-for-byte; manifests, digests and cited observations all resolve.

## What is running, and how to run it

| Script | Purpose | Guards |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `run-campaign.ps1 -Concurrency N` | capture families in parallel | refuses `N>1` for any navigator but `prisoner` |
| `run-arena.ps1` | the save-mutating arena route | refuses to start without a fresh snapshot, takes it itself, hashes before/after |
| `launch-capture.ps1` | one session; the ONLY script with both `-WatchFields` and `-Stage*` | **no snapshot guard** — see Open items |
| `validate-vehicle.ps1` | wrapper gate after any edit | prints the source hash it compiled, and what it does not prove |
| `save-state.ps1` | snapshot/restore | refuses an empty tree, and refuses to restore a WIPED save |

Snapshots: **`level4-vitality-tournament-gate`** (vitality 13, 5723 gold,
`current_tournament` 1 — `hitpointsmax` reads 220 in the level-up log because
`battlevalues` last ran pre-spend; the formula gives 300) and
**`level4-armed-weapon39`** (the same gladiator after a shop trip: weapon 39,
843,130 gold, strength and speed 60). `verified-good-1701` and `pre-arena-path`
are the original level-1 gladiator.

**`zainger-repaired` is a WIPED save under a reassuring name.** `save-state.ps1`
now refuses to restore it without `-Force`.

## Non-negotiable rules (each learned the hard way)

- Licensed SWFs are read-only and hash-verified before and after every capture.
  Never copy, export or commit game assets or extracted scripts.
- **Never shortcut the game's own frames.** Jumping past the prologue tripped
  the game's own validation screen.
- A candidate becomes golden ONLY via >=2 matching observations from >=2
  sessions. Never hand-write a golden, observation or manifest.
- **Derive candidates from the battle map, never from a capture.**
- `validate-vehicle.ps1` must PASS after ANY wrapper edit — but see below for
  what that does and does not mean.
- Snapshot before every save-mutating run. `run-arena.ps1` does it for you.
- Use `git commit -F <file>` for any message containing quotes.

### AVM1 has ONE comparison opcode

`>` is `<` with operands swapped; `>=` and `<=` are `<` negated. Every
comparison with NaN is false, so **both negated forms return TRUE for NaN**, and
every field the wrapper reads is undefined until the frame that initialises it.
This caused **three separate live defects in one day**, including one that
rewrote the gladiator's gold. The only safe shape is un-negated `<`, twice:
`(n < 1) || (0 < n)`. Use `isNum()`.

### `validate-vehicle.ps1` proves less than its name suggests

Audited: it catches **0 of the 6 defects found live on this route**, `isNum` has
**zero reachable call sites** in a stub run, and a one-line revert of `isNum`'s
body leaves the gate green while restoring the demonstrated save-corruption bug
verbatim. Save corruption is outside its observable universe by construction —
it compares a trace to a fixture, never a save. It now says so in its own PASS
output and names the wrapper source hash it compiled.
